# Sources

The tracked companies, where the candidates came from, and why every other
candidate was dropped. Generated by `jobpipe recon-select` from the live checks `checks/results/recon-harvest-001/`, `checks/results/recon-verify-001/` (raw per-board results are in `verify.csv` there).

## Totals per provider

| Provider | Companies | Open jobs at recon | US jobs at recon |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| greenhouse | 779 | 43,988 | 36,583 |
| lever | 52 | 2,781 | 2,464 |
| ashby | 669 | 17,551 | 15,304 |
| **total** | **1,500** | **64,320** | **54,351** |

## Seed sources

| Source | What it gave | Link |
|---|---|---|
| Common Crawl URL index, crawls `CC-MAIN-2026-43`, `CC-MAIN-2026-39`, `CC-MAIN-2026-34`, `CC-MAIN-2026-30`, `CC-MAIN-2026-25`, `CC-MAIN-2026-21`, `CC-MAIN-2026-17`, `CC-MAIN-2026-12` | board names from every captured URL on boards.greenhouse.io, job-boards.greenhouse.io, jobs.lever.co and jobs.ashbyhq.com | https://index.commoncrawl.org/ |
| Y Combinator company directory, via the yc-oss JSON mirror | 473 active, hiring, US companies with ≥ 15 staff (of 6362); up to 3 guessed board names each, tried on all three providers | https://www.ycombinator.com/companies , https://yc-oss.github.io/api/companies/all.json |

Harvest: 9,904 distinct (provider, board) candidates. Verify: 9,904 API calls, one per candidate.

## Selection rules (approved 2026-11-03)

1. The API answered 200 with at least 1 listed job and at least 1 US role.
2. Mostly US: ≥ 50% of jobs with a known country are in the US, and the country is known for ≥ 50% of jobs.
3. Tech company: ≥ 40% of titles match the tech-title signal (`src/jobpipe/recon/signals.py`).
4. Companies only: staffing/recruiting agencies, job aggregators and test/demo boards are dropped (rules in `src/jobpipe/recon/select.py` plus the reviewed list in `seeds/exclusions.csv`).
5. One board per company: when a company is on two boards, the one with more open jobs is kept and noted.
6. At most 1,500 companies (ranked by US tech roles); at least 1,000 required, all three providers represented.

Every candidate that was not kept, with its reason. Summary:

| Reason | Boards |
|---|---:|
| board not found | 3,542 |
| not a tech company | 1,837 |
| 0 US roles | 985 |
| mostly non-US | 744 |
| no open jobs | 573 |
| below the 1,500-company cut | 554 |
| country unknown for most jobs | 136 |
| duplicate | 22 |
| job aggregator | 7 |
| staffing or recruiting agency | 3 |
| test or demo board | 1 |
